We prove this objective is Bayes-aligned with the stopping target and that score-only rules are suboptimal under heterogeneous costs. We evaluate on 1,343 tasks across five tool-use domains. On $τ$-bench Retail, CAM-DF attains the highest payoff among deployable methods, with gains over a predict-then-threshold baseline across all five ranking sources and two cost regimes. Our approach is state-of-the-art under heterogeneous costs and high cost pressure, with larger gains under weaker rankings. In live execution, CAM-DF exposes the agent to 37\% fewer tools than full access while maintaining comparable task success. The CAM-DF family is a lightweight pre-execution plugin that turns existing tool rankings into lower-cost acquisition decisions without fine-tuning the underlying LLM.

</details>


### [212] [Sky sphere representation in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27092)
*Aleksandr Berdnikov,Yevgeny Liokumovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现约100B参数的语言模型在残差流中编码了可解码的夜空地图表示，该表示具有弯曲的高维不可约特征流形，中位角度误差低至12°-21°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型（~100B参数）是否在内部表征中编码了关于夜空的空间知识，以及这种表征是否可被解码提取。

Method: 对多个开源模型进行探针分析，从残差流中解码夜空地图表示；使用LOO（留一法）测试验证表示的有效性；通过主成分分析（PCA）观察表示在相关提示下的涌现；并验证该表示不是来自相关扁平表示的简单泄露。

Result: 大多数开源模型确实含有可解码的夜空表示；在LOO测试中表现显著，R²分数达65-85%，中位角度误差为12°-21°；该表示出现在提示相关问题时残差流的前几个主成分中。

Conclusion: 首次发现了弯曲的高维不可约特征流形（curved high-dimensional irreducible feature manifold），表明LLM内部可能存在更复杂的几何结构来编码知识。

Abstract: We analyze whether language models of size ~100B have a representation of the night sky map that is decodable from their residual stream. We find that most of the considered open-source models do have such a representation, and it often even surfaces to the top principal components on prompts that ask questions like ``what is close to this object in the night sky''. In all but one model this representation showed significant scores in LOO testing, containing up to 65-85% of variance ($R^2$-score) and having median angular error down to $12^\circ-21^\circ$. We verify that our representation is not a simple leak from a correlated flat representation. To our knowledge, this representation is the first example of a curved high-dimensional irreducible feature manifold.
  Codes used in the paper are published at https://github.com/l3erdnik/Decodable-sky

</details>


### [213] [Hierarchical Spatio-Temporal Transformer for Coherent Emergency Department Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27106)
*Filipa Lino,Bárbara Tavares,Carlos Santiago,Cláudia Soares,Manuel Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HierSTT，一个基于层次化Transformer的框架，用于对急诊科(ED)就诊需求进行多层级（医院、区域、国家）协同预测，在葡萄牙81家医院的数据集上相比非层次化深度学习基线降低32%的WAPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ED需求预测大多在单一层级独立进行，忽略了医院-区域-国家之间的层级结构，导致预测结果不一致（即医院级预测无法聚合为区域/国家级需求）。

Method: 提出HierSTT框架：1) Temporal Fusion Transformer捕获国家级动态；2) 时空Transformer编码器-解码器模块以高层级预测为条件建模区域和医院需求；3) 引入一致性损失函数惩罚跨层级预测不一致性。

Result: 在葡萄牙81家医院、5个区域的数据集上，HierSTT相比最佳非层次化深度学习基线降低32%的WAPE，优于所有经典层次协调方法，并生成近乎一致的跨层级预测。

Conclusion: 层次化多层级协同预测方法能有效解决ED需求预测中的不一致性问题，显著提升预测精度。

Abstract: Emergency Departments (EDs) are critical access points in healthcare systems, yet they face persistent pressure from unpredictable patient demand, seasonal surges, and non-urgent visits. Effective ED planning requires forecasts at multiple decision-making levels: hospitals need local demand estimates for staffing and bed management, regions require forecasts to coordinate healthcare units, and national authorities need system-wide projections for capacity planning. However, most existing approaches forecast ED demand independently at a single level, ignoring the hierarchy linking hospitals, regions, and national systems. This can produce incoherent predictions, where hospital-level forecasts do not aggregate consistently to regional or national demand. We propose HierSTT, a hierarchical Transformer-based framework for coherent multi-level ED forecasting. HierSTT jointly predicts hospital, regional, and national level demand in a single end-to-end model. A Temporal Fusion Transformer captures national dynamics, while spatio-temporal Transformer encoder-decoder modules model regional and hospital demand conditioned on higher-level forecasts. A coherence-aware loss penalizes cross-level inconsistencies during training. We further introduce a nationwide Portuguese ED dataset covering 81 hospitals across 5 regional health administrations, with heterogeneous covariates at each level. Experiments show that HierSTT reduces average WAPE by 32\% relative to the best non-hierarchical deep learning baseline and outperforms all classical hierarchical reconciliation methods, while producing near-coherent predictions across levels. Additional resources associated with this work are available at https://github.com/FilipaLino/HierSTT.

</details>


### [214] [Voronoi Histograms for Adaptive Vectorization of Expected Persistence Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27126)
*Kaifeng Zhang,Kai Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Voronoi直方图的期望持续图向量化方法，在无需显式光滑变换的前提下实现拓扑特征的稳定表示，并在分类与降维任务中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EPD向量化依赖预定义的光滑点变换（如高斯函数或景观函数），计算成本高且灵活性受限。本文旨在探索一种无需显式光滑模型的离散化替代方案。

Method: 采用Voronoi图直方图作为EPD的向量化工具，用基于自适应分区的计数方法替代光滑函数逼近。在分离性和归一化条件下建立了稳定性界，并刻画了直方图表示保留Wasserstein尺度变化的条件。

Result: 在具有显著拓扑特征的真实数据集上，所提表示在分类和降维任务中表现有效。

Conclusion: Voronoi直方图是一种不依赖光滑点变换模型的EPD向量化方法，在保证稳定性的同时具备良好的实用性能。

Abstract: Persistence Diagram (PD) is known to capture point cloud topology effectively, but its computation has high time complexity. Expected Persistence Diagram (EPD) has been developed to reduce the time cost by studying the topology of multiple subsets of a point cloud and it serves as a distribution of topological features. Existing EPD vectorizations often rely on predefined point transformations, such as Gaussian or landscape functions. We study an alternative discretization based on Voronoi histograms, which trades smooth functional approximation for adaptive partition-based counting. We propose to use Voronoi Diagram-based histogram as the vectorization of EPD, without imposing an explicit smooth point transformation model. Under stated separation and normalization conditions, we establish stability bounds and characterize when the histogram representation preserves Wasserstein-scale variation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed representation on real-world datasets which have significant topological features for classification and dimensionality reduction tasks.

</details>


### [215] [Minimal Markovization via Stable Quotients in Holonomy-Cover Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27132)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Yongshan Chen,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了holonomy-cover POMDP中的最小马尔可夫充分统计量——稳定商（stable quotient），并提出了Holonomy Memory RL方法，在实验中用3个记忆状态实现了完美决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境中，智能体需要维护一个可递归更新的历史统计量来恢复马尔可夫性，但最小统计量通常未知，本文旨在刻画这一最小统计量。

Method: 构造稳定商（coarsest observation-wise abstraction），证明当前观测与稳定类的配对构成精确的有限马尔可夫状态；提出Holonomy Memory RL，通过有序边传输更新稳定类，并在可诊断时识别局部类坐标，同步后使用标准有限MDP算法。

Result: 实验从原始状态精确压缩到商状态，用三个决策时间记忆状态实现完美配对顺序准确率，匹配商oracle并优于非oracle基线。

Conclusion: 稳定商是holonomy-cover POMDP的最小马尔可夫充分统计量，基于此的Holonomy Memory RL能高效恢复状态并进行标准有限MDP学习。

Abstract: An agent acting under partial observability must retain a recursively updateable statistic of history that restores the Markov property, but the smallest such statistic is generally unknown. We characterize this minimal Markov sufficient statistic for holonomy-cover decision processes, a structured POMDP class in which the visible dynamics are Markov and every realized visible transition applies a fixed permutation to a hidden mode. In particular, we construct the stable quotient, the coarsest observation-wise abstraction preserving one-step rewards and quotient successors, and prove that the pair of the current observation and stable class forms an exact finite Markov state. When the current class is correctly initialized, exact class tracking requires exactly the minimal memory symbols, in the sense that under reachability and pairwise decision separation at a maximizing observation, no arbitrary finite-memory controller can use fewer. Under resettable diagnostics, nearest-prototype class inference has exponentially decaying error, and a calibrate-then-restart reduction transfers finite-MDP guarantees to the recovered state. The results enable \emph{Holonomy Memory Reinforcement Learning}. It represents memory by the current stable class, updates it through ordered edge transports, identifies local class coordinates when diagnostics are available, and applies a standard finite-MDP RL backbone after synchronization. Experiments recover an exact compression from raw states to quotient states and achieve perfect paired-order accuracy with three decision-time memory states, matching the quotient oracle and outperforming the non-oracle baselines.

</details>


### [216] [Cost-Sensitive Conformal Prediction and Human-in-the-Loop Abstention for Imbalanced High-Stakes Decision Support: A Multi-Domain Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27143)
*Manpreet Singh,Akshatha Srikantha,Shyamal Lakhanpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了边际共形预测、Mondrian共形预测和成本控制拒绝机制在不平衡数据集上的表现，发现Mondrian CP能将少数类覆盖率平均提升61.7个百分点，结合成本控制拒绝可显著降低高风��决策成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险决策系统（如信用评分、欺诈检测等）面临严重类别不平衡和不对称错误成本，标准边际共形预测虽然提供整体覆盖保证，但在少数类上覆盖极低（低至0.5%），存在严重缺陷。

Method: 在15个真实不平衡表格数据集上，使用7种分类模型、3种概率校准技术和10个随机种子，共3,150次实验，系统比较了边际CP、Mondrian CP和成本控制拒绝机制。

Result: Mondrian CP平均将少数类覆盖率提升61.7个百分点（p < 1e-80）；结合成本控制拒绝后，在现实人工审查预算下显著降低期望决策成本；确定了数据集特定的盈亏平衡阈值。

Conclusion: Mondrian CP结合成本控制拒绝机制为高风险决策系统提供了分布自由、成本感知的不确定性量化实用指导。

Abstract: High-stakes decision systems in credit scoring, fraud detection, healthcare, and industrial safety require reliable uncertainty quantification under severe class imbalance and asymmetric error costs. Standard marginal conformal prediction (CP) provides valid overall coverage guarantees; however, we show that it severely under-covers rare, costly minority classes, with minority-class coverage dropping to as low as 0.5% on certain datasets. To characterize and address this limitation, we conduct a comprehensive benchmark comparing marginal CP, class-conditional (Mondrian) CP, and cost-controlled abstention mechanisms across 15 real-world imbalanced tabular datasets, 7 classification models, 3 probability calibration techniques, and 10 random seeds, resulting in 3,150 experimental runs. Our results show that Mondrian CP restores valid minority-class coverage, achieving an average minority-coverage improvement of 61.7 percentage points over marginal CP (p < 1e-80). Furthermore, combining Mondrian CP with cost-controlled abstention significantly reduces expected decision cost compared with standard decision boundaries, confidence-based rejectors, and risk-controlled rejectors under realistic human review budgets. We further quantify dataset-specific break-even thresholds at which deferring ambiguous instances to human experts becomes cost-effective. These findings provide practical guidance for deploying distribution-free, cost-aware uncertainty quantification in high-stakes decision support systems.

</details>


### [217] [Skillful forecasting of offshore winds from satellite scatterometer constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27152)
*Francesco Pinto,Luca Lanzilao,Paco Lopez Dekker,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WindCastNet是首个基于卫星散射计观测数据的近海风速风向临近预报框架，利用部分卷积LSTM网络处理时空不规则的多源卫星数据，在北海区域测试中相比数值天气预报模型在1小时和2小时前瞻时间分别降低23%和7%的均方根误差，为短临海上风电预测提供了独立且具有竞争力的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近海风电预报依赖数值天气预报，但NWP模型对分钟到小时级别的短临预报（初始条件精度主导）并非最优，而卫星散射计数据虽被同化进NWP却从未被直接用于预报，因此亟需一种能直接从卫星观测中学习并处理其时空不规则性的预报方法。

Method: 提出WindCastNet框架，采用部分卷积长短期记忆（LSTM）网络，利用欧洲、中国和印度散射计星座的微波雷达观测数据。通过编码空间观测掩码和观测间隔，并引入连续时间表征，实现对不规则空间覆盖、异步采样和可变重访时间的处理，支持任意前瞻时间的预报。

Result: 在北海区域评估中，WindCastNet在1小时和2小时前瞻时间相比HARMONIE MEPS模型分别降低23%和7%的RMSE；在前三小时预报中优于持续性预报9-15%；但在强风条件和空间非均匀流场下预报技能下降。

Conclusion: 卫星散射计星座可作为独立且具有竞争力的短临海上风电预报来源，开辟了可再生能源预测的新路径，并可扩展到热带气旋临近预报等更广泛的海洋天气应用。

Abstract: Accurate intraday forecasts of offshore wind are becoming increasingly important for power system operation and the integration of growing shares of offshore wind energy. Operational forecasts rely predominantly on numerical weather prediction (NWP), which is not optimized for lead times of minutes to hours, where initial-condition accuracy dominates forecast skill. Although satellite scatterometer observations are routinely assimilated into NWP, they have not previously been used directly for forecasting. Here we present WindCastNet, the first satellite-based nowcasting framework for offshore wind speed and direction, introducing a new paradigm for intraday forecasting that learns from spatiotemporally irregular satellite observations. WindCastNet predicts offshore wind fields from observations acquired by satellite scatterometer constellations. WindCastNet employs a partial convolutional long short-term memory network that exploits microwave radar observations from the European, Chinese, and Indian scatterometers despite their irregular spatial coverage, asynchronous sampling, and variable revisit times. Spatial observation masks and inter-observation intervals are encoded, while a continuous temporal representation enables forecasts at arbitrary lead times. Evaluated over the North Sea, WindCastNet reduces the root-mean-square error by 23% and 7% relative to the HARMONIE MEPS model at lead times of 1 and 2 h, respectively, and outperforms persistence by 9-15% during the first three forecast hours. Forecast skill decreases under strong-wind conditions and spatially non-uniform flow. These results demonstrate that satellite scatterometer constellations can provide an independent and competitive source of short-term offshore wind forecasts, opening new opportunities for renewable energy forecasting but also broader marine weather applications, including tropical cyclone nowcasting.

</details>


### [218] [When Do Learned Diffusion Proposals Help Constraint Solving? A Controlled Study on Continuous Algebraic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27169)
*Quang Bui,Sparsh Roy,Akash Gundimeda,Davin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARC框架，将连续代数约束系统转化为因子图，使用图神经网络扩散去噪器提出赋值、计算机代数能量精化、符号验证器验证。研究发现学习型提议仅在特定情况下优于随机多起点搜索，实际系统中经典多起点普遍更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决连续代数约束系统时，经典求解器擅长值的选择，但结构增强只能通过枚举进行，效率低下。需要一种能同时处理值决策和结构增强的方法，并系统评估学习型方法相较于随机搜索的实际增益。

Method: 将连续代数约束系统转化为因子图，用图神经网络扩散去噪器提出赋值，通过精确的计算机代数能量进行精化，最后由精确符号验证器认证解。候选条件修复排序器（K次增强）替代穷举搜索。

Result: 候选条件修复排序器在平衡非线性菜单准确率上达0.997（随机0.236），p<10^-70；学习型提议仅在特定高维区域优于随机多起点，通过1-(1-q(n))^K公式可完美预测随机多起点成功率（MAE 0.012）；8个真实系统中经典多起点解决所有问题。

Conclusion: 学习型提议对求解器的改进存在明确的适用区间：在高维区域有效，但变量耦合时优势消失；实际系统大多不在学习有利区间内；随机多起点搜索以1-(1-q(n))^K为精确数学模型，无需自由参数。

Abstract: Solving a continuous algebraic constraint system requires two decisions: which values satisfy the constraints, and which structural augmentation renders an unsolvable system solvable. Classical solvers answer the first well and the second only by enumeration. On that discrete decision, a candidate-conditioned repair ranker choosing among K augmentations reaches the exhaustive-search ceiling at a fraction of the calls, outperforming random (0.997 vs 0.236 balanced nonlinear menu accuracy; p < 10^-70; 0.982 +/- 0.006 across seeds) and beating a budget-matched per-candidate probe on accuracy and cost. MARC turns such a system into a factor graph, over which a graph-neural diffusion denoiser proposes assignments, descent on an exact computer-algebra energy polishes them, and an exact symbolic checker certifies solutions. Evaluations of diffusion-based proposals rarely include one control: random multi-start under the same refinement budget. Applied to our system, it sharply curtails what the learned proposal contributes on the value decision. Does it beat random multi-start at choosing satisfying assignments? Only narrowly, in a predictable regime. Across trapped low-dimensional families it ties with random restart, but dominates in high dimension, where random search fails. Once variables couple, the advantage is gone. Since all methods share one polish and one checker, best-of-K random multi-start succeeds with probability exactly 1 - (1 - q(n))^K, where q(n) is single-start reachability; one measured constant, with no free parameters, reproduces the entire curve (mean absolute error 0.012). The favorable regime is not specific to our synthetic families: across eight real-world systems in robotics, positioning, optimization, and algebra, classical multi-start solved all eight, none in the learning-favorable regime. We map the regimes in which learned proposals improve solvers.

</details>


### [219] [Inverse Learning of Latent Risk-Neutral Densities from Irregular Option Quotes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27188)
*Lennon J. Shikhman,Michael Galarnyk,Aadi Dash,Nicholas A. Welsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 准确的期权价格不代表能准确恢复隐含的风险中性密度。本文通过两个基准实验发现：双成分对数正态混合模型在综合误差上表现最佳，而学习型算子（DeepONet、报价Transformer）在特定误差指标上更有优势。数值条件分析揭示了价格与密度间的病态映射，支持目标相关的归纳偏置，而非存在通用最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究准确期权价格与准确恢复隐含风险中性密度之间的区别，揭示二者并非等价关系。

Method: 设计两个互补基准：控制基准（已知真实密度）和时间序列NIFTY基准（仅基于市场价格）。比较双成分对数正态混合、DeepONet、报价Transformer等多种方法在价格误差、密度误差等多维度上的表现，并进行数值条件分析。

Result: 双成分对数正态混合在综合误差上最低；DeepONet在1%分位数和方差误差上分别降低39.0%和34.6%；报价Transformer在Merton族上降低L1误差16.4%；数值分析显示95/126个定价方向为数值零方向，两个密度即使L1差距0.061也会产生相同价格。

Conclusion: 不存在通用的最优方法，应选择与目标匹配的归纳偏置（目标相关归纳偏置）。

Abstract: Accurate option prices do not imply accurate recovery of the latent risk-neutral density. We study this distinction with two complementary benchmarks. A controlled benchmark exposes simulator-truth densities for latent evaluation, while a chronological NIFTY benchmark tests only held-out market prices. A two-component lognormal mixture has the lowest aggregate price, $L^1$, Wasserstein, and fixed-tail errors on the synthetic benchmark. Learned operators retain narrower strengths: DeepONet reduces 1% quantile and variance error by 39.0% and 34.6% relative to the mixture, and a quote transformer reduces $L^1$ by 16.4% on the structurally misspecified Merton family. A numerical conditioning analysis explains why these rankings can differ: after enforcing mass and forward constraints, 95 of 126 pricing directions are numerically null, and two densities separated by $L^1 = 0.061$ produce identical prices on the covered strikes. On 524 held-out NIFTY calls, validation-selected test-time adaptation reduces DeepONet RMSE by 28.3%, but per-expiry mixture and SVI fits remain much more accurate. The evidence supports target-dependent inductive bias, not a universal winner.

</details>


### [220] [From Classification to Regression: Using a Fruitfly to Solve Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27196)
*Shady E. Ahmed,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受果蝇感知机制启发，提出一种将回归任务转化为分类问题的框架，通过存储局部模式并基于相似度加权重建进行预测，适用于动力系统、数据回归和物理信息学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局代理模型在处理复杂非线性输入输出关系时计算和存储成本高，而科学数据往往只占据输入空间中有限且重复的区域，因此有必要设计一种更高效、可控制精度与成本权衡的方法。

Method: 构建一个有限局部模式库替代全局模型；离线阶段从数据或控制方程中提取模式，在线阶段仅需计算查询与存储模式的相似度，然后通过加权重建组合对应的响应来生成预测。

Result: 该方法在非线性动力系统、数据驱动回归和物理信息学习任务上均适用；通过在线/离线分离显著降低了计算和内存需求，并实现了精度、存储量和推理成本之间的显式控制。

Conclusion: 受果蝇感知启发的分类式回归框架为科学计算中的非线性问题提供了一种灵活、高效且可控的替代方案，尤其适合处理数据分布有限且重复的场景。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to regression tasks using classification which is motivated by the mechanism used by fruitflies to sense their environment. Specifically, we formulate a general framework for learning nonlinear input-output relationships by replacing complex global surrogate models with a finite library of representative local patterns. Since scientific data often occupy limited and recurring regions of the input space, we generate predictions by measuring similarities between a query and stored patterns, then combining their associated responses through weighted reconstruction. We apply this approach to nonlinear dynamical systems, data-driven regression, and physics-informed learning using suitable embeddings and similarity measures. For dynamical systems, our offline-online workflow extracts patterns from data or governing equations during the offline phase, while online prediction requires only similarity evaluation and response aggregation. This structure helps us reduce computational and memory demands while providing explicit control over the trade-off among accuracy, storage, and inference cost.

</details>


### [221] [Do You Really Need to Pretrain Q-Functions for Online RL Fine-Tuning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27203)
*Perry Dong,Ron Polonsky,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Fin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在基于价值函数的强化学习中，预训练Q函数是否真的有助于微调。意外发现，简单的Q函数预训练相比于随机初始化几乎没有优势，原因是预训练学到的Q函数与在线微调收敛的Q函数之间存在根本性不匹配。作者提出策略集成初始化（IPE）方法，通过训练多个多样化策略并进行池化回放来引导在线RL中的Q函数学习，在连续控制基准测试中相比预训练方法提升了1.26倍微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在预训练策略之上进行微调时，预训练Q函数是否能真正带来性能提升，以及解释为何随机初始化Q函数有时反而表现更好这一反直觉现象。

Method: 提出Initialization via Policy Ensemble (IPE)方法，训练多个多样化策略，利用它们收集的池化回放数据来引导在线强化学习中Q函数的学习，解决预训练Q函数与在线微调Q函数之间的不匹配问题。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的连续控制基准测试中，IPE相比朴素Q函数预训练方法，微调性能平均提升1.26倍。

Conclusion: 预训练Q函数因目标不匹配问题对微调帮助有限，IPE通过策略集成的方式有效缓解了这一不匹配，实现了更好的微调性能。

Abstract: Pre-training followed by fine-tuning has become the dominant recipe for learning performant policies, and in value-based reinforcement learning (RL) this raises a natural question: given a pretrained policy, should the Q-function be pretrained on offline data too? Conventional wisdom suggests it should, but recent results show that online RL with a randomly-initialized Q-function can result in highly performant and reliable policies without needing to pretrain the Q-function. In this paper, we systematically study whether pretraining the Q-function actually helps when fine-tuning on top of a pretrained base policy. We find, surprisingly, that naive Q-function pretraining often provides little benefit over random initialization. We show this stems from a fundamental mismatch: the Q-function learned during pretraining targets the pretrained policy's Q-function, not the Q-function that online fine-tuning converges to, and this gap persists even after offline value maximization. Motivated by this finding, we propose Initialization via Policy Ensemble (IPE), a simple method that trains multiple diverse policies and uses their pooled rollouts to bootstrap the Q-function learning in online RL. Across a suite of challenging continuous control benchmarks, IPE yields an average 1.26x improvement in fine-tuning performance over naive Q-function pre-training.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [A Persona-based Rate Action Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26545)
*Hayden Helm,Andrew Dassori*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"数字人格"集合的指数，用于预测美联储FOMC的利率决策。通过收集近25,000条公开数据构建每位成员的数字人格，发现这些人格能高度识别对应成员（识别准确率是随机水平的8倍），生成内容与真实内容几乎无法区分。该指数在2022-2025年间有效追踪利率周期（Kendall's τ=0.68），可构建简单分类器预测会议结果（准确率0.69 vs 基准0.47），且领先联邦基金利率约三个季度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以捕捉FOMC成员随时间变化的集体立场和决策倾向，作者希望利用大语言模型构建多个"数字人格"来模拟不同成员对市场条件的反应，从而预测利率决策。

Method: 收集近25,000条公开数据，按FOMC成员划分语料库；为每位成员构建一个基于检索增强生成的"数字人格"系统；通过可识别性（成员归属准确率）和可检测性（生成内容与真实内容的区分度）评估人格真实性；利用查询条件下的表征捕捉成员货币政策立场；构建基于人格的利率行动指数。

Result: 人格的成员条件召回率是随机水平的8倍；生成内容检测指标τ̂_det=0.23（基准0.15）；人格表征与鹰鸽声誉排序的Kendall's τ=0.63；利率指数与利率周期的Kendall's τ=0.68；分类器预测准确率0.69（基准0.47）；指数领先联邦基金利率约三个季度。

Conclusion: 首次证明了通过数字人格集合捕捉时变群体行为的能力，该方法在预测FOMC利率决策方面优于信息基线，具有实用价值。

Abstract: We propose an index for predicting the U.S.\ Federal Open Market Committee (FOMC) decision to hike/hold/cut the current federal funds target rate based on how a collection of personas responds to current market conditions. To construct the index, we collected a new dataset consisting of nearly $25{,}000$ retrievable chunks from publicly available data. We partition the data into per-member corpora and use each as the retrieval database of a generative system we refer to throughout as a ``persona''. We first evaluate the personas across two complementary components of likeness: identifiability and detectability. Each persona's behavior is highly attributable (average member-conditional recall is $ 8\times $ chance) and generated content is nearly indistinguishable from held-out real content ($\hatτ_{\mathrm{det}} = 0.23$ against a $0.15$ floor). We then present evidence that query-conditioned representations of the personas capture members' monetary-policy stance relative to a known hawk--dove reputational ordering (Kendall's $τ= 0.63$, $p < 0.001$), substantially outperforming retrieval-only representations. These representations vary with time and current market conditions and form the basis of our proposed persona-based rate action index. For the $2022$--$2025$ period the index tracks the rate cycle (Kendall's $τ= 0.68$, $p < 10^{-6}$) and can be used to construct a simple classifier that predicts per-meeting outcomes at non-trivial accuracy ($0.69$ versus a $0.47$ base rate). Importantly, the index outperforms informative baselines and leads the federal funds target rate by roughly three quarters. As far as we are aware, our results are the first to demonstrate the ability to capture time-varying group behavior via a collection of digital personas.

</details>


### [223] [One Run Is Not an Idea: The Implementation Lottery in Automated Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26587)
*Jingjie Ning,Shanshan Zhong,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自动化研究系统中的“实现抽签”问题：一个想法的实验得分依赖于具体实现版本，导致想法层面的结论不可靠。作者提出想法可靠性审计方法，通过多个独立实现来评估想法本身的可靠性，而非仅依赖单一实现的分数。实验表明，实现方差远大于重跑同一实现的方差，且基于单一实现选出的最优想法在多个实现间经常反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化研究系统使用实验得分来评估和选择想法，但每个得分仅对应一个具体实现。这种将实现层面的分数直接当作想法层面的证据，会导致“实现抽签”问题——结论取决于采样到了哪个实现。需要量化这个问题的严重性并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出“想法可靠性审计”（Idea Reliability Audit）方法：验证并冻结候选卡片，采样全新的独立实现，使用不看结果的忠实度标签，重跑保存的工件。报告想法ICC（组内相关系数）和留一实现（LOO）胜者反转率。在13个表格任务和两个编码智能体设置上进行了312次分配实验。

Result: 实现方差分别是重跑同一工件方差的5倍（表格任务）和10倍（编码任务）。基于单一实现选出的胜者在其他实现下平均反转率达25.6%（表格）和43.6%（编码）。在两个不看结果的审核规则下，反转现象仍然存在。在三个材料回归工作流中，实现变异同样主导了方差分解。

Conclusion: 想法的可靠性不同于最佳N个工件的效用。在得分用于指导想法层面的分支、迁移或研究记忆之前，证据应覆盖多个独立实现。研究者应区分“想法可靠性”和“工件效用”，避免因实现抽签得出误导性结论。

Abstract: Automated research systems use experimental scores both to deliver artifacts and to decide which ideas to retain, transfer, and pursue. Yet one run scores one implementation of an idea. Crediting that realization-level score as evidence about the parent mechanism creates the \emph{implementation lottery}, in which an idea-level conclusion depends on which plausible implementation was sampled. The mismatch is structural whenever one run updates beliefs about a mechanism. We estimate its magnitude. The \emph{Idea Reliability Audit} measures \emph{idea reliability} by validating and freezing candidate cards, sampling fresh-session implementations, using outcome-blind fidelity labels, and rerunning saved artifacts. It reports idea ICC and leave-one-implementation-out (LOO) winner reversal. Prior work generally repeats the task; we repeat the idea. Across 312 assignments on 13 tabular tasks and two coding-agent setups, implementation variance was more than five and ten times same-artifact rerun variance, respectively, and the winner from one implementation draw differed from the winner under the other-two mean in 25.6\% and 43.6\% of decisions. Reversal survives card-level filtering under two outcome-blind review rules. An exploratory diagnostic on three materials-regression workflows with a deterministic evaluator also finds implementation variation dominating the decomposition. These findings distinguish idea reliability from best-of-$N$ artifact utility. Before a score guides idea-level branching, transfer, or research memory, evidence should cover multiple implementations.

</details>


### [224] [Living-Harness Is an Interactive-Agent Evolver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26598)
*Yuetian Du,Yucheng Wang,He Xu,Jiexu Xu,Shanwen Tan,Bing Zhao,Boyu Yang,Zhijie Xu,Ming Kong,Hu Wei,Jie Liu,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出Living-Harness，一种自我进化的智能体框架，通过将每次交互轨迹和评估信号转化为证据，持续更新智能体的程序性知识（包括情景记忆和状态图），从而减少重复性失败，提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在失败后虽可重试，但由于固定不变的交互框架（harness）无法从经验中学习，同类错误会在不同任务中反复出现，缺乏持续改进机制。

Method: 设计域级别的Evolution-SOP指导更新过程：从完整轨迹中提取抽象摘要和结构化证据，写入两种程序性知识——情景记忆（记录触发条件、失败模式、恢复动作）和状态图（记录状态节点、修复边和转移规则），在保持工具和基础上下文不变的前提下，迭代更新harness状态。

Result: 在τ²-Bench和MultiWOZ-2.4衍生出的8个交互环境中，Living-Harness相比最强交互基线在Pass@1上分别提升10.07和9.91个百分点，且进化后的harness状态可在不同模型间通过检索方式复用。

Conclusion: Living-Harness通过让智能体框架具备自我进化能力，有效缓解了重复性失败问题，显著提升了任务成功率，并展现了良好的跨模型迁移性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents may recover from a failure within an episode or after a retry, yet the same execution failure can recur in later tasks because post-episode feedback rarely revises the persistent harness that guides future interactions. Static harnesses improve reliability through fixed tools, context, memory, and workflow structures, but remain unchanged after deployment. We propose $\textbf{Living-Harness}$, a self-evolving agent harness that converts each completed trajectory and its evaluator signals into posterior evidence for bounded harness updates. Guided by a domain-level $\textbf{Evolution-SOP}$ ($\textbf{S}$tandard $\textbf{O}$perating $\textbf{P}$rocedure), Living-Harness extracts an episode abstraction and structured update evidence, and writes two complementary forms of procedural knowledge: episodic memory that records trigger conditions, failure patterns, and recovery actions, and a state graph that records state nodes, repair edges, and transition rules. The updated harness state is retrieved to guide future interactions, while tools and base context remain frozen, allowing procedural repairs to accumulate across evolution cycles. On eight interactive environments derived from $τ^2$-Bench and MultiWOZ-2.4, Living-Harness improves average Pass@1 over the strongest interactive baseline by 10.07 and 9.91 percentage points, respectively, and supports retrieval-only reuse of the evolved harness state across model backbones.

</details>


### [225] [DREvo: Distilling Recalibrated Historical Experience for Harness Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26722)
*Hanghui Guo,Weijie Shi,Zhangze Chen,Shengxiang Xu,Yishu Wang,Yimei Zhang,Wangze Ni,Jia Zhu,Shimin Di*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出DREvo方法，通过动态证据重校准和搜索意图蒸馏，解决了LLM agent框架（harness）自我进化中历史经验利用不稳定、性能波动大的问题，在有限进化预算下显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有harness自我进化方法存在两个局限：(1)缺乏对历史经验在当前harness中是否仍然有效的动态重评估；(2)缺乏将有效历史经验转化为可操作搜索方向的显式机制。导致性能波动大，难以在有限预算下可靠发现高性能harness。

Method: 提出DREvo方法，包含三个核心组件：(1)函数级证据锚定（function-level evidence anchoring）；(2)状态依赖的证据重校准（state-dependent evidence recalibration）；(3)角色条件化的搜索意图蒸馏（role-conditioned search intent distillation）。用于判断哪些历史证据仍然有效，以及harness下一步应如何进化。

Result: 在有限进化预算下，DREvo展现出更平滑的进化轨迹，在全部5个基准测试中取得最高准确率，在领域推理任务上平均提升16.2%，在智能体任务上平均提升14.2%。

Conclusion: DREvo通过动态重评估历史经验和显式引导搜索方向，有效解决了harness自我进化中的性能波动问题，在有限预算下实现了更稳定且更优的性能。

Abstract: Harness plays a critical role in large language model agent performance, and building a high-performing harness requires substantial expert effort. Therefore, recent research has increasingly explored harness self-evolution, which iteratively proposes, evaluates, and improves harnesses using historical trial experience. However, accumulated historical experience does not always translate into stable search guidance, and performance often fluctuates substantially across evolution iterations, making it difficult to reliably discover high-performing harnesses under a limited evolution budget. We identify two limitations in how existing harness self-evolution methods leverage historical experience: (1) Lack of dynamic reassessment of whether historical experience remains valid for the current harness, and (2) Lack of explicit mechanisms for translating valid historical experience into actionable search directions. To address these limitations, we propose a new harness self-evolution method, named DREvo, which integrates function-level evidence anchoring, state-dependent evidence recalibration, and role-conditioned search intent distillation to determine which historical evidence remains valid and where the harness should evolve next. Under limited evolution budgets, DREvo exhibits smoother evolution trajectories, achieves the highest accuracy on all five benchmarks, and delivers average gains of 16.2% and 14.2% over the evaluated baselines on domain reasoning and agentic tasks, respectively.

</details>


### [226] [Cross-organisational Process Mining from Message Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26917)
*Pieter Kwantes,Jetty Kleijn*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从消息日志（记录进程间通信）而非事件日志（记录单个进程内部行为）出发，通过将消息日志转换为保留因果顺序的事件日志，进而合成异步通信系统（Industry nets）的建模方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中，跨组织获取事件日志（记录内部执行动作）往往困难或不可行，而记录进程间消息交换的消息日志更容易获取。因此需要探索如何仅利用消息日志来构建通信系统的模型。

Method: 研究如何将消息日志（消息交换的记录，包括输出和输入事件）转换为一种保留消息事件间因果顺序的事件日志，再沿用先前工作[21,22]中的算法，从该事件日志合成Industry net（异步通信系统模型）。

Result: 提出了一种将消息日志转换为事件日志的方法，使得转换后的事件日志能够保留原始消息事件之间的因果顺序关系，从而可以进一步用于Industry net的合成。

Conclusion: 基于消息日志也能有效合成异步通信系统的模型，为在难以获取内部事件日志的情况下进行业务流程发现提供了可行的替代途径。

Abstract: Enterprise nets and Industry nets are specific Petri net models that together form a framework for the modelling of collaborating business processes and the specification of interactions between them. In this set-up, individual business process are modelled as Enterprise nets, while Industry nets consist of Enterprise nets that interact by exchanging messages through unidirectional channels. In previous contributions (see [21,22]), it was established how to leverage algorithms for the discovery of single processes (modelled as Enterprise nets) from event logs (consisting of finite sequences of executed actions), to synthesise systems consisting of asynchronously communicating processes (modelled as Industry nets). However, in general, it may be difficult to work with event logs from different organisations or even get access to them. Recordings of message exchanges between processes may be easier available.Therefore, we focus in this paper on the communications between different processes and turn to the investigation of message logs, \ie collections of recordings of communications in the form of message exchanges (output and input events) between two processes. In particular, the question is addressed of how a message log can be transformed into a description of the communication behaviour in the form of an event log such that the causal order on message events is preserved. Following the approach from [21,22], this event log can then be used to synthesis an Industry net.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Probing the Origins of Reasoning Performance: Representational Quality for Mathematical Problem-Solving in RL vs. SFT Fine-Tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26119)
*Antyabha Rahman,Akshaj Gurugubelli,Omar Ankit,Kevin Zhu,Aishwarya Balwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过线性探针和均值消融实验，发现RL训练使模型在数学推理中形成更线性可分、层级化的隐藏层表征，而SFT模型表征均匀分布。但token分配差异更多取决于训练流程而非RL与SFT本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强化学习训练的大推理模型在数学推理任务上优于监督微调模型的内部表征机制差异。

Method: 1) 基于层间隐藏状态的线性探针预测答案正确性；2) 均值消融实验分析各层重要性；3) 重复采样下token数量变异性分析以评估自适应计算分配。

Result: RL模型隐藏状态的线性可分离性更强，层次架构中深层逐渐更重要；SFT模型重要性均匀分布；token分配变异性在不同RL模型中表现不一致。

Conclusion: RL训练从根本上重构了模型表征和推理方式，但token分配差异更多取决于整体训练流程，而非单纯RL与SFT的对立。

Abstract: Large reasoning models trained via reinforcement learning (RL) have been increasingly shown to outperform their supervised fine-tuned (SFT) counterparts on mathematical reasoning tasks; Yet the mechanistic basis for this advantage remains unclear. We therefore ask, what internal representational differences enable RL models' superior performance? Our work presents two converging lines of evidence: First, linear probes trained on layer-wise hidden states reveal that RL models tend to achieve higher accuracy in predicting answer correctness compared to SFT models, indicating more linearly separable and structured representations. Second, mean ablation studies show that RL models develop a hierarchical architecture where deeper layers become progressively more critical, whereas SFT models distribute importance uniformly across layers. Together, these findings demonstrate that RL training fundamentally restructures how models represent and process reasoning problems. Finally, we analyze token-count variability under repeated sampling across problems to assess adaptive compute allocation. While we observe higher variability in some RL-tuned models than in their SFT counterparts, we see strong consistency in others, suggesting that token allocation may depend more on the overall training pipeline than on RL versus SFT alone. We believe this token-allocation variability reveals the spread of plausible on-policy reasoning, highlighting which models exhibit stable policies versus those that are under-determined, potentially non-identifiable solution behaviour.

</details>


### [228] [Even More Deception: Objective Misalignment in Mixed-Motive LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26120)
*Marylou Fauchard,Florian Carichon,Margarida Carvalho,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用社交推理游戏《狼人杀》构建了一个评估LLM多智能体系统中目标错位（objective misalignment）的框架，通过修改单个智能体的目标但保留其角色身份，分析了四个模型系列、四种角色和三种目标设定下的内部推理、公开廉价谈话行为和游戏结果。研究发现目标错位会严重破坏整体表现，且这种影响在信息不对称和角色专业化的环境中更加显著——虽然被操控的智能体会发展出独特的目标导向推理策略，但这些策略在公开行为中几乎不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM驱动的多智能体系统中，智能体常处于混合动机环境（mixed-motive environments），面临信息不对称和策略性欺骗，导致目标与集体目标错位的问题日益突出。现有研究缺乏系统性的评估框架来衡量这种目标错位对集体决策的影响。

Method: 采用《狼人杀》这一社交推理游戏作为实验环境，通过修改单个智能体的目标（同时保留其角色身份），对四种不同模型系列/规模的LLM、四种玩家角色和三种目标设定进行实验。采用双重分析：内部推理分析（智能体的推理过程）和公开廉价谈话行为分析（无成本、非约束性沟通），并辅以游戏结果分析。

Result: 目标错位会损害本已具有对抗性环境中的游戏结果，且信息不对称和专业化角色会加剧这种负面影响。被操控的智能体会发展出与目标相关的独特推理策略，但这些策略在公开行为中几乎不可见。

Conclusion: 即使是微妙的目标错位也会深刻影响集体决策，亟需为基于LLM的多智能体系统开发有效的缓解策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs)-powered multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed in mixed-motive environments, where agents operate under asymmetric information and strategic deception due to conflicting or hidden objectives. In these settings, misalignment with collective goals becomes a central concern. We propose a novel framework for evaluating objective misalignment using the social deduction game Werewolf, modifying the objective of a single agent while preserving its assigned role. Across LLMs from four different model families and sizes, four player roles, and three objective formulations, we introduce a dual analysis of the agents' internal reasoning and their public cheap-talk behavior (i.e costless, non-binding communication that does not directly affect the agents' utilities), complemented by an analysis of game outcomes. Our results show that objective misalignment undermines outcomes in inherently adversarial environments, an effect exacerbated by asymmetric information and specialized roles. While compromised agents consistently develop distinct objective-dependent reasoning strategies, these adaptations remain largely invisible in their public behavior. More broadly, our findings suggest that even subtle objective misalignment can profoundly affect collective decision-making, highlighting the need for effective mitigation strategies for LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [229] [ClinLens: Towards Long-Horizon Coding Agents for Longitudinal Multimodal Clinical Data Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26155)
*Yuan Zhu,Ethan B. Liu,Frank Nie,Jindong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLINLENS是一个包含200个可执行任务的临床数据分析基准，跨越5个MIMIC资源（结构化电子病历、临床笔记、心电图、胸片、超声心动图），采用4×5分类法。最强模型-脚手架配置在126个任务子集上仅达56.3%的严格通过率，而执行成功率100%，揭示可运行提交与正确临床分析之间的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准孤立处理医学问答、结构化表格推理或通用科学数据，缺乏能评估临床数据科学智能体端到端分析能力的综合性基准。

Method: 构建CLINLENS基准（200个可执行任务），采用4×5分类法（4种患者时间范围×5种分析能力），通过程序优先的反向合成方法生成任务，每个任务包含半原始数据包和评估器私有的参考工作流。

Result: 在固定126任务集上，最强配置（24种模型-脚手架组合中）严格通过率56.3%（执行成功率100%）；单独配置的编码智能体解决83/126任务，而基于GPT-4o-mini的5个生物医学系统最多仅达2.9%严格通过率。

Conclusion: 当前临床数据科学智能体虽然能执行任务，但在正确性上存在显著不足，亟需提升分析正确性而非仅仅可运行性。

Abstract: Clinical data-science agents must transform heterogeneous longitudinal records into auditable analyses, yet existing benchmarks largely isolate medical question answering, structured-table reasoning, or generic scientific repositories. We introduce CLINLENS, a benchmark of 200 executable tasks over five linked MIMIC resources spanning structured electronic health records, notes, electrocardiograms, chest radiographs, and echocardiograms. A 4 x 5 taxonomy crosses four patient-time scopes with five analysis capabilities. Program-first reverse synthesis pairs each bounded semi-raw package with an evaluator-private reference workflow and checks required artifacts, cohort and temporal semantics, and the final answer. On a fixed 126-task suite, the strongest of 24 standardized model-scaffold configurations achieves 56.3% scope-macro STRICTPASS despite 100% EXECSUCCESS. For reference, a separately configured coding agent solves 83 of 126 tasks, while five biomedical systems adapted to GPT-4o-mini reach at most 2.9% scope-macro STRICTPASS. These results expose a substantial gap between runnable submissions and correct clinical analyses.

</details>


### [230] [Partner Capability Estimation for Task-Agnostic Adaptation in Ad-Hoc Teamwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27177)
*Peter Tisnikar,Maja Swieczkowska,Benteng Ma,Gerard Canal,Matteo Leonetti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CE-CM（基于上下文模型的能力估计）方法，将临时团队协作扩展到多任务设置下，通过近似贝叶斯推理推断任务无关的能力向量，并引入CE-CM-Div扩展以应对人类行为多样性，实验证明该方法能快速恢复隐藏能力、减少不可行动作分配并适应动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临时团队协作方法大多假设单一固定任务且已知合作伙伴能力，但现实中合作伙伴的真实能力常是隐藏的，且人类可能在不同有效策略下行动次优。本文旨在解决这些局限性，将临时团队协作扩展到多任务场景。

Method: 将临时团队协作重新定义为隐藏能力下的联合规划与分散执行问题；提出CE-CM近似贝叶斯方法，通过仿真采样推断任务无关的能力向量；构建上下文多智能体MDP进行规划；提出CE-CM-Div扩展，用多样化规划器轨迹替代单一最优轨迹来评估能力假设。

Result: 模拟实验表明CE-CM能快速恢复隐藏能力、减少不可行动作分配并适应动态变化；在15名参与者的225条轨迹离线人类研究中，CE-CM-Div相较于基线CE-CM显著提升了能力估计效果。

Conclusion: 基于能力建模的方法在研究的设置中是一种有前景的、可解释的、任务无关的表征方式，考虑行为多样性对于实现稳健的人机协作至关重要。

Abstract: Effective collaboration with novel and diverse partners is a crucial skill for autonomous agents. Most current ad-hoc teamwork (AHT) approaches assume that agents will collaborate on a single, fixed task and that the partner's capabilities, their ability to successfully execute the desired action, are already known. In reality, a partner's true capabilities are often hidden, and human collaborators may act sub-optimally on tasks with multiple valid strategies. To address these limitations, we extend ad-hoc teamwork into a multi-task setting by re-framing it as a problem of joint planning with decentralised execution under hidden partner capabilities. We introduce CE-CM (Capability Estimation via Contextual Models), an approximate Bayesian method that infers task-invariant capability vectors. By using simulation-based sampling, the agent estimates capabilities and induces a contextual Multi-agent Markov Decision Processes for planning. This approach requires no population pre-training and refines its beliefs online from just a few tasks. To account for human unpredictability, we propose CE-CM-Div, an extension that evaluates capability hypotheses against diverse planner rollouts rather than a single optimal trajectory. Simulated experiments demonstrate that CE-CM rapidly recovers hidden capabilities, reduces infeasible action assignments, and adapts to changes over time. Furthermore, in an offline human study of 225 trajectories from 15 participants, CE-CM-Div substantially improved capability estimates over the baseline CE-CM method. Our results suggest capability-based modelling is a promising interpretable, task-agnostic representation in the studied settings, demonstrating that accounting for behavioural diversity is essential for robust human-AI teaming.

</details>


### [231] [When benchmark inferences do not compose: Projectibility in AI evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26159)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出AI基准测试结果无法直接得出有意义的结论，需要经过多次推论才能转化为实际主张。作者提出"非组合原则"：即使相邻推论的证据各自有效，也不能自动保证链条的整体有效性。通过法律研究案例、再分析和模拟，展示了基准到实际应用的论证中常见的逻辑断裂问题，并提出了"可投射性审计"来诊断这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以效度为中心的方法要求每个主张都有证据支持，但忽略了"有根据的链接不能自动构成有根据的链条"这一更深层的认知问题——相邻推论各自有效并不意味着组合后依然有效。

Method: 借鉴古德曼的"竞争扩展"问题与基于论证的效度理论，提出"非组合原则"；通过法律研究案例展示问题，并利用再分析和模拟说明聚合稳定性如何抹消后续投射所需的关键区分。

Result: 发现基准证据与部署研究可能各自有效却保持平行（无法对接）；聚合稳定性会掩盖后续推论所需的关键差异。

Conclusion: 提出"可投射性审计"（projectibility audit）作为诊断基准到实际应用论证中"无效连接"（unsupported joins）的工具。

Abstract: An AI benchmark result rarely reaches a consequential claim in one step. Evaluators generalize it to further cases, interpret it as evidence of capability, extrapolate it to new tasks, transport it to another system or site, and combine it with assumptions about human review and downstream consequences. Validity-centred approaches require evidence for each claim. This paper identifies a further epistemic problem: warranted links don't automatically make a warranted chain. The target of one study may not be the source of the next; system, population, outcome, or conditions may change at the interface; and shared data or model lineage may make apparently independent support dependent. Projectibility concerns whether a bounded extension from observed to unobserved cases is warranted. Goodman supplies the problem of rival extensions; argument-based validity supplies an architecture for testing them. The paper's distinctive claim is a non-composition principle: support for adjacent projections warrants their composition only when endpoints and assumptions align and dependence and uncertainty are carried through. A legal-research case shows how benchmark evidence and a deployment study can each be sound while remaining parallel. A reanalysis and simulation show why aggregate stability can erase distinctions a later projection requires. The resulting projectibility audit diagnoses unsupported joins in benchmark-to-use arguments.

</details>


### [232] [GuideSkill: Evolving Executable LLM Agent Skills for Guideline-Grounded Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26160)
*Lang Cao,Yuhao Shen,Tianyang Luo,Simo Du,Hao Peng,Yue Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GuideSkill，一个将临床指南编译为可执行函数的外部推理层，包含从指南初始化的GuideSkill-Zero和从病例数据演化的GuideSkill-Evo。在四个基准和四个骨干模型上，GuideSkill-Zero相比指南RAG平均提升13.45%宏平均准确率，GuideSkill-Evo在无需更新骨干模型的情况下，相对直接推理提升18.49%，且黄金标准技能覆盖率从56.5%提升至99.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM系统处理临床指南时通常只是检索文本或通过训练吸收知识，而非真正执行指南中的规则逻辑，导致诊断准确性和可解释性不足。

Method: GuideSkill构建外部推理层，将疾病特定标准编译为返回有序诊断支持得分的可执行函数。GuideSkill-Zero从指南初始化技能，GuideSkill-Evo利用病例-诊断对优化技能并补充缺失诊断。推理时LLM提出鉴别诊断，将所需特征与匹配技能对齐，融合排序结果与技能执行得分。

Result: GuideSkill-Zero相比指南RAG平均提升13.45%宏平均准确率。GuideSkill-Evo在四个骨干模型上均达到最高宏平均准确率，相对直接推理提升18.49%，黄金标准技能覆盖率从56.5%提升至99.5%。在Qwen3.5-9B上超过最强参数更新基线11.16%。专家评估表明其技能临床合理且可接受。

Conclusion: 可执行技能可作为模型无关的机制，有效结合指南推导的程序与病例推导的诊断模式，具有可靠性和实际意义。

Abstract: Clinical practice guidelines (CPGs) encode diagnostic criteria, but LLM systems typically retrieve guideline text or absorb it through training rather than execute its rules. We introduce GuideSkill, an external reasoning layer that compiles disease-specific criteria into executable functions returning ordinal diagnostic-support scores. GuideSkill-Zero is initialized from guidelines, while GuideSkill-Evo uses case--diagnosis pairs to refine covered skills and add missing diagnoses. At inference, an LLM proposes a differential diagnosis, grounds the features required by each matched skill, and fuses its ranking with the executed skill scores. Across four benchmarks and four backbones, GuideSkill-Zero improves macro-average accuracy over guideline RAG by 13.45% on average. GuideSkill-Evo achieves the highest macro-average for every backbone, improves over direct inference by 18.49% relatively, and increases gold-label skill coverage from 56.5% to 99.5%. On Qwen3.5-9B, it also exceeds the strongest parameter-update baseline by 11.16% without updating the backbone. Expert evaluation further indicates that GuideSkill produces clinically sound and broadly acceptable skills, suggesting that its initialized and evolved rules are reliable and practically meaningful. These results support executable skills as a model-agnostic mechanism for combining guideline-derived procedures with case-derived diagnostic patterns.

</details>


### [233] [GoGoTB: Agentic RTL Verification with Specification-Grounded Coverage Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26181)
*Xin Xin,Jincheng Lou,Junhui Li,Jinglin Yan,Panda Xiao,Di Wu,Haixiao Li,Weicong Lu,Weijian Fan,Xinyu Qu,Yuxiang Zhao,Min Yu,Zhixiong Di,Yibo Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GoGoTB是一个端到端的LLM智能体框架，通过三层子系统（执行控制层、知识系统、覆盖率框架）实现了完整的IC验证闭合，在8个RTL设计上无需人工干预达到100%环境生成成功率和平均98.4%行覆盖率等优异指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM验证方法采用独立单轮调用，缺乏共享上下文，导致接口不匹配无法检测、覆盖率与规范要求脱节，而功能验证占IC前端工程主要工作量且一次漏检bug可能引发昂贵的芯片重制。

Method: 提出GoGoTB智能体框架，包含三个子系统：1) 执行控制层——在每个工具和阶段边界将确定性强制执行与LLM推理分离；2) 可进化知识系统——按需调度方法论和设计特定专业知识；3) 规范导向的覆盖率闭合框架——将每个覆盖率仓锚定到命名的规范行为，使每个残留缺口都有可诊断的根本原因和针对性修复方案。

Result: 在8个RTL设计上无需人工干预，达到100%环境生成成功率，平均行覆盖率98.4%、分支覆盖率97.2%、翻转覆盖率97.0%、功能覆盖率83.2%。此前没有任何工作能在相同基准上成功生成完整验证环境或达到有意义覆盖率。

Conclusion: GoGoTB首次以完全自动化的方式实现了LLM驱动的端到端验证闭合，有效解决了接口不匹配和覆盖率脱节两大核心问题，在多个RTL设计上取得了接近甚至超过人工水平的验证指标。

Abstract: Functional verification dominates integrated circuit (IC) front-end engineering effort, and a single missed bug that escapes to silicon can trigger a costly respin. Recent large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to automate this process, yet existing LLM-based approaches generate each component through independent single-turn calls with no shared context, leaving interface mismatches undetected and reported coverage disconnected from specification requirements. To address these challenges, we present GoGoTB, an agentic framework that achieves end-to-end verification closure through three subsystems: an agentic execution control layer, an evolvable knowledge system, and specification-grounded coverage closure. The execution control layer separates deterministic enforcement from LLM reasoning at every tool and stage boundary. The knowledge system dispatches methodology and design-specific expertise on demand. The coverage framework anchors every bin to a named specification behavior so that each residual gap has a diagnosable root cause and a targeted remedy. Tested on 8 register transfer level (RTL) designs without any human intervention, GoGoTB achieves 100\% environment generation success and averages 98.4\% line, 97.2\% branch, 97.0\% toggle, and 83.2\% functional coverage. No prior work successfully generates a complete verification environment or achieves meaningful coverage on the same benchmarks.

</details>


### [234] [Position: Evaluation Scores Are Perishable Knowledge Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26191)
*Sankalp Gilda,Shlok Gilda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有语言模型评估方法通过平均化聚合多种信号（自动指标、LLM评判、人工评估、基准测试）会导致“信任膨胀”，即评估置信度超过最弱信号的可信度。论文提出评估结果应被视为具有形式性、范围和有效期三个属性的认知主张，并基于链式思维分析、可能逻辑和代数理论，提出最弱链聚合作为保守端点。通过HELM排行榜实证表明，按平均分和按最弱链排名前五的模型完全不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前语言模型评估中平均化聚合多种信号导致的信任膨胀问题，即评估置信度被虚假放大，超过最弱信号的实际可靠性。

Method: 结合链式思维分析、可能逻辑和代数理论三种研究传统，推导出最弱链聚合作为参数化算子族（由单一悲观参数控制）的保守端点。并提出评估结果应附带显式元数据（形式性层级、范围声明、有效期）。

Result: 在HELM公开排行榜上，54个前沿模型在10个场景中，按平均分排名前五的模型与按最弱链排名前五的模型完全不相交，验证了平均化聚合的严重偏差。

Conclusion: 评估应转向最弱链聚合而非平均值，并引入形式性、范围、有效期等元数据以透明化评估的认知状态。

Abstract: Evaluation methodologies for language models increasingly combine multiple signals, from automated metrics and LLM-as-judge ratings to human assessments and benchmark suite results. When these signals are aggregated via averaging, evaluation confidence can then substantially exceed the reliability of the weakest signal: a phenomenon we call trust inflation in evaluation. We argue that evaluation scores should be treated as epistemic claims with three properties: formality (human evaluation provides stronger evidence than an automated metric), scope (a benchmark result applies to the tested distribution, not universally), and validity windows (benchmark results expire as contamination accumulates and distributions shift). Several converging research traditions (chain-of-thought analysis, possibilistic logic, and algebraic theory) establish weakest-link aggregation as the conservative endpoint of a parameterized operator family controlled by a single pessimism parameter. Drawing on those traditions, and on concrete lessons from building an evaluation harness for agentic AI, we propose that evaluation results carry explicit metadata (formality tier, scope declaration, and expiration date) to make their epistemic status transparent. We illustrate the cost of mean aggregation on the public HELM leaderboard: across 54 frontier models on ten scenarios, the top-five models ranked by mean score and by weakest-link are completely disjoint.

</details>


### [235] [TraceCoder: Explainable and Auditable Code Generation with Position-Key Snippet Versioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26307)
*Rwaida Alssadi,Muntaser Syed,Balaji Kasula,Lamine Deen,Majed Alotaibi,Mohammed Alghamdi,Tyler Ton,Ali Alqarni,Marius Silaghi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TraceCoder 提出了一种基于关系型代码片段历史记录、浏览器可视化工具和竞争性分数位置键索引的可审计代码生成方法，使LLM-based编码智能体的黑箱输出变得可追溯、可审计、可回放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM编码智能体将代码作为黑箱输出，每行代码背后的推理逻辑不可见，基准驱动修复过程中代码的演变过程是短暂的，事后审计不可能。这阻碍了自动化代码生成在生产部署中的可信度与问责性。

Method: (i) 关系型代码片段历史模式：记录每个修复事件的基准引用、轮次编号、失败文本和LLM解释，支持完整溯源查询；(ii) 基于浏览器的可视化工具：将历史渲染为热力图、悬停注释的源代码；(iii) 竞争性分数位置键索引方案：使用树节点分隔符为每个代码片段分配稳定、字典序排序的标识符，实现细粒度追踪而不扰乱周围代码行。

Result: 在30个算法编程任务上评估，其中10个在6轮迭代内耗尽预算。平均Chg%达30%，3/10的代码片段带有可追踪的修复事件行（在Gemini 2.0 Flash上20个任务子集中为21%）。三个详细案例展示了系统如何解释哪些基准失败塑造了最终程序的每一行。

Conclusion: TraceCoder使自动化代码生成的内部"叙事"变得可审计和可回放，这对生产部署中的信任和问责性至关重要。

Abstract: Contemporary LLM-based coding agents produce code as black-box outputs: the rationale behind each line is hidden, the evolution of the code through benchmark-driven repair is ephemeral, and post-hoc auditing is impossible. We present a code generation concept that addresses these shortcomings through three complementary mechanisms: (i) a relational snippet-history schema that records, per repair event, the benchmark reference, round number, failure text, and LLM explanation, enabling full provenance queries; (ii) a browser-based visualisation tool that renders this history as heat-mapped, hover-annotated source code; and (iii) a competitive fractional position-key indexing scheme with tree-node delimiters that assigns stable, lexicographically-ordered identifiers to each code snippet, enabling fine-grained tracking without disrupting surrounding lines. We evaluate TraceCoder on 30 algorithmic programming tasks spanning string processing, mathematical computation, and data-structure manipulation, across two provider configurations. Of these, 10 exhaust the 6-iteration budget on tasks with subtle edge-case behaviour. Mean Chg% reaches 30%, three in ten code snippets carry a traceable repair-event row, compared to 21% when using Gemini 2.0 Flash as sole provider on a 20-task subset. Three detailed case studies demonstrate how the system explains which specific benchmark failures shaped each line of the final program. The proposed mechanism makes the internal "narrative" of automated code generation auditable and replayable, a property essential for trust and accountability in production deployments.

</details>


### [236] [Exploring Structures in Physics Problems: Can AI Agents Discover Statistical Mechanical Mappings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26367)
*Wanyu Zhao,Wanbing Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了StatMechBench-v0基准，用于评估LLM智能体在统计力学中识别可解模型映射的能力。实验表明智能体能通过数值检查修复代码，但常误判计算复杂度或可解类型，需要超越数值验证的符号检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI智能体是否能够像理论物理学家一样，将新问题转化为已知可解模型，从而探索LLM在结构性发现任务上的能力边界。

Method: 构建了包含六种伊辛类问题的StatMechBench-v0基准（覆盖转移矩阵、规范可移除无序、平面/Pfaffian结构），采用提出-验证-修正（propose-verify-revise）代理框架，在多种LLM和问题表述下进行系统评估。

Result: 数值反馈能帮助智能体修复代码并恢复正确的配分函数，但智能体常在通过数值检查的情况下误判底层可解类或低估计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 当前LLM推理存在局限性，未来AI智能体对理论物理的结构性发现需要超越数值一致性检查的验证栈，融入符号检查和结构不变量。

Abstract: An important skill in theoretical physics is to recognize when a new problem can be transformed into a known model. We study this skill as an AI-agent task: can LLM-based agents discover statistical mechanical mappings from a raw partition function to a tractable representation? To probe this question, we introduce StatMechBench-v0, a benchmark of six Ising-type problems covering transfer-matrix methods, gauge-removable disorder, and planar/Pfaffian structure. We evaluate a simple propose-verify-revise agent across multiple LLMs and problem phrasings. The results show that numerical feedback often helps agents repair code and recover correct partition functions. However, agents can also pass the numerical checks while misidentifying the underlying tractable class or understating computational complexity. This both reveals limitations in current LLM reasoning and calls for a verification stack that goes beyond numerical agreement, incorporating, for example, symbolic checks and structural invariants. Our study provides an early evaluation and design directions for AI agents aimed at structural discovery in theoretical physics.

</details>


### [237] [CaM-Wolf: Causal-Aware Multimodal Agents for Social Deduction Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26393)
*Zheng Zhang,Nanjie Yao,Jiarui He,Deheng Ye,Peilin Zhao,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CaM-Wolf是首个整合多模态感知与生成的社会推理游戏AI智能体，通过视频输入处理、因果推理训练和动画化身呈现，在游戏表现和人机交互质量上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会推理游戏AI多基于纯文本，忽略了人类社交中多模态交互这一核心要素，亟需弥合这一差距。

Method: 1) 多模态感知：处理其他玩家的视频输入；2) 因果推理器：通过强化学习训练，建立可观察行为与隐藏角色之间的逻辑链条；3) 动画化身：以动画形式呈现自身。

Result: CaM-Wolf在游戏表现和用户研究均优于基线方法，实现了更优的智能体游戏性能，并提升了人机交互质量。

Conclusion: 该工作是向能够参与复杂社交动态的类人AI智能体迈出的重要一步。

Abstract: Social deduction games (SDGs) such as Werewolf have become challenging testbeds for AI agents. These games require complex social skills such as reasoning, deception, and collaboration. While recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have driven significant progress in SDG agents, current approaches are predominantly text-based, overlooking the multimodal nature that is fundamental to human social interaction. To bridge this gap, we introduce CaM-Wolf, the first SDG agent that integrates multimodal perception and generation. CaM-Wolf processes video inputs from other players, employs a causal-aware Reasoner trained via reinforcement learning to establish logical chains between observable behaviors and hidden roles, and presents itself through an animated avatar. Our experiments and user study show that CaM-Wolf achieves superior agent gameplay performance and enhances the quality of human-AI interaction. This work represents a significant advancement towards creating more human-like AI agents capable of participating in nuanced social dynamics. Our code is available at https://3dagentworld.github.io/avatar_wolf.

</details>


### [238] [CG-World: A Large-Scale World-State Dataset and Protocol for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26452)
*Yiming Cai,Fangjie Yu,Meiqing Yu,Ziyue Shi,Pengfei Yuan,Yong Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CG-World是一个从工业CG管线中提取的大规模世界状态数据集，包含约85万个时空对齐的样本，涵盖多模态语义、空间结构、运动、物理、事件等结构化信息，并支持事实轨迹、观察/动作/机制干预及反事实推理分支。实验表明其在视频生成、动作预测和具身策略迁移中具有重用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频、机器人、仿真数据集通常只捕获状态、动作、事件、观测中的部分结构，缺乏完整的联合动态建模能力，因此需要构建一个显式记录中间状态及干预分支的大规模世界状态数据集。

Method: 从工业计算机图形学生产管线中提取结构化世界状态数据，包含多模态语义、空间结构、骨架与控制器状态、运动曲线、相机与光照参数、物理缓存、接触事件和多通道渲染。数据集定义了事实轨迹、观察干预、动作干预、机制干预和严格反事实分支，并显式记录干预目标、不变量和替代结果。

Result: 在几何条件视频生成、动作预测和闭环视觉-语言-动作策略迁移任务上验证，结果表明CG-World为可控生成、动作建模和具身策略迁移提供了可复用的结构化监督信号。

Conclusion: CG-World为世界模型、物理AI和具身智能提供了一个共享的数据基础设施，团队计划通过持续数据采集和社区协作扩展该数据集。

Abstract: World models must learn the joint dynamics of states, actions, events, and observations, yet existing video, robotics, and simulation datasets usually capture only part of this structure. We introduce CG-World, a large-scale world-state dataset and protocol derived from industrial computer graphics production pipelines. CG-World explicitly records intermediate states, including multimodal semantics, spatial structure, skeletal and controller states, motion curves, camera and lighting parameters, physics caches, contact events, and multi-pass renderings. CG-World v1 contains approximately 850,000 temporally aligned segments of 1-5 seconds. It separates latent states, observations, relations, events, and branch metadata, and organizes them into unified spatiotemporal samples. To support intervention learning and counterfactual reasoning, CG-World defines a branch lineage covering factual trajectories, observation interventions, action interventions, mechanism interventions, and strict counterfactual branches, with intervention targets, invariants, and alternative outcomes explicitly recorded. We evaluate the dataset on geometry-conditioned video generation, action prediction, and closed-loop vision-language-action policy transfer. Results show that CG-World provides reusable structured supervision for controlled generation, action modeling, and embodied policy transfer. We plan to expand CG-World through continued data collection and community collaboration toward a shared data infrastructure for world models, Physical AI, and embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [239] [MultivationBench: A Benchmark for Multimodal Sequential Motivation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26465)
*Kawai Chung,Chunkit Chan,Yauwai Yim,Yuxuan Liu,Haochen Shi,Weiqi Wang,Qing Zong,Tianshi Zheng,Yixuan Fu,Kai Chung Wong,Hao Liang,Yifan Gao,Xi Yang,Janet Hui-wen Hsiao,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MultivationBench基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在故事驱动的视觉叙事中进行序列动机推理的能力，发现现有模型在跨上下文的动态推理上存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要考察静态文本或孤立视觉快照，无法反映真实世界行为驱动的累积性，因此需要构建能评估序列动机推理的多模态基准。

Method: 基于Maslow需求层次和Reiss基本欲望等心理学框架，构建故事驱动的视觉叙事基准MultivationBench，要求模型整合累积的多模态上下文来推断演化的动机。

Result: 所有测试模型在序列上下文中的动机推理一致性均表现不佳，暴露出静态识别能力与人类式动态推理之间的关键脱节。

Conclusion: MultivationBench揭示了当前多模态大语言模型在动态动机推理方面的显著短板，为未来提升模型的社会智能提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have sparked significant interest due to their potential for social intelligence; however, their ability to perform sequential motivation reasoning remains insufficiently studied. Existing evaluations predominantly examine static text or isolated visual snapshots, which do not reflect the cumulative nature of real-world behavioral drivers. To address this gap, we introduce MultivationBench, a benchmark designed to rigorously evaluate multimodal motivation reasoning within story-driven visual narratives. The benchmark builds upon established psychological frameworks - Maslow's hierarchy and Reiss's basic desires - and requires models to integrate accumulated multimodal context to infer evolving motivations. Results indicate that MultivationBench presents a significant challenge: all tested models struggle to maintain consistent motivation reasoning across sequential contexts, revealing a critical disconnect between static recognition capabilities and the dynamic reasoning essential for human-like social understanding.

</details>


### [240] [EvoPINN: Agentic Discovery of Executable Algorithms for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26490)
*Peng Yin,Kai Li,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoPINN框架，利用LLM智能体自动搜索神经表示与训练程序的模块化空间，通过执行验证自动发现适用于PDE求解的专用学习算法，并自主发明了新架构SLRC-PINN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN的性能严重依赖人工试错式的网络设计、损失函数和优化策略，现有LLM自动生成代码方式在科学计算约束下常产生无效解，亟需一种自动化、可执行的算法发现方法。

Method: EvoPINN将神经表示与训练程序解耦，构建模块化搜索空间；LLM智能体基于记忆迭代提出程序化修改；所有候选方案经过结构验证和预算匹配的PDE评估，确保科学有效性。

Result: 在振荡型、椭圆型、耗散型和非线性输运等多类PDE上，EvoPINN发现的学习算法显著降低了相对L2误差；自主发明的SLRC-PINN架构在参数严格匹配下仍保持性能优势。

Conclusion: 执行验证的智能体能够自主发现真正新颖的科学计算机制，为PINN自动化设计提供了可行的新范式。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), yet their performance heavily relies on the manual, trial-and-error engineering of neural representations, loss formulations, and optimization dynamics. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for automated design, unconstrained code generation often yields mathematically invalid or numerically unstable solutions under strict scientific computing constraints. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{EvoPINN}, an agentic framework that reformulates PINN development from labor-intensive manual design into a rigorous, execution-grounded algorithm discovery problem. EvoPINN navigates a modular search space by decoupling neural representations from training programs, utilizing an LLM agent to iteratively propose memory-conditioned programmatic modifications. To ensure scientific validity, all candidates undergo strict structural verification and budget-matched PDE evaluation. Extensive experiments across diverse PDE regimes (oscillatory, elliptic, dissipative, and nonlinear transport) demonstrate that EvoPINN discovers PDE-specialized learning algorithms that significantly reduce relative $L_{2}$ error compared to baselines. Crucially, EvoPINN autonomously invented SLRC-PINN, a novel architecture whose performance gains persist under rigorous parameter-matched comparisons, establishing the viability of execution-grounded agents for discovering genuinely new scientific computing mechanisms.

</details>


### [241] [Evidence-Ledger Adjudication for Claim-Evidence Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26512)
*Gengyu Chen,Yongjie Yu,Weiling Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种“证据分类账判定”方法，通过将每个声明与证据包配对并分配支持关系，实现AI辅助写作中声明的可追溯性和可审计性。在包含2,335条标注数据的基准测试中，该方法在关系准确率（0.676）和宏F1（0.601）上显著优于非Agent基线，并能有效路由需要人工核查的声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理生成声明的速度远超作者验证证据的速度，现有工作缺乏声明与证据之间的可追溯性和可审计性流程。

Method: 提出证据分类账判定工作流：将每个声明与证据包配对→分配支持关系→将无支持、矛盾或混合证据的声明路由回作者。在AVerTeC、CLIMATE-FEVER、SciFact上构建2,335条盲审基准测试，隐藏金标准标签进行预测，最后才联合评分。

Result: Agent证据分类账条件：关系准确率0.676，宏F1 0.601；最佳非Agent基线：准确率0.383，宏F1 0.303。路由了1270/1435条本应路由的声明，同时路由了295/900条本不该路由的声明。

Conclusion: 证据分类账判定能将异质证据包转化为可审计的追溯层，显著优于传统方法，为AI辅助写作提供了可靠的声明验证机制。

Abstract: AI agents can draft claims faster than authors can check whether the cited or retrieved evidence supports them. We study evidence-ledger adjudication: a claim-evidence traceability workflow that pairs each claim with an evidence packet, assigns a support relation, and routes unsupported, contradicted, or mixed-evidence claims back to the author. The empirical core is a 2,335-row blind benchmark built from independent external labels in AVeriTeC, CLIMATE-FEVER, and SciFact. Gold relations and source evidence labels are hidden during prediction and joined only for scoring. On this benchmark, the agent evidence-ledger condition achieves 0.676 relation accuracy and 0.601 macro-F1, compared with 0.383 accuracy and 0.303 macro-F1 for the best non-agent baseline. It also routes 1270/1435 claims whose gold labels indicate contradiction, missing evidence, or mixed evidence, while routing 295/900 supported claims. These results show that evidence-ledger adjudication can turn heterogeneous evidence packets into an auditable traceability layer for AI-assisted writing.

</details>


### [242] [Eco3S: Complex Socio-Economic System Simulation via Agent-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26588)
*Shaopeng Wei,Yufei Cheng,Wenxi Sun,Yepeng Ding,Yu Zhao,Gang Kou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Eco3S框架，通过共演化环境设计、结构因果模拟和仿真-分析-精炼范式，解决LLM-based ABM在演化交互、反事实推理和工作流自动化方面的挑战，在多个经济场景中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的智能体建模面临三大挑战：建模演化的智能体-环境交互、灵活的反事实推理、以及科学研究的自动化仿真工作流。现有方法难以兼顾这三方面需求。

Method: 提出Eco3S框架，包含三个关键机制：(1)共演化环境设计——智能体与环境双向反馈循环；(2)结构因果模拟——基于SCM的反事实机制，支持灵活干预；(3)仿真-分析-精炼范式——自我纠正机制，迭代优化实验设计。

Result: 在多个经济场景（运河衰退、治理起源、信息传播）中成功复现已有经济研究和现象，并验证了框架的可扩展性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: Eco3S框架为严谨的经济研究和政策制定提供了有效的仿真工具，能够解决当前LLM-based ABM的关键挑战。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has renewed interest in agent-based modeling (ABM). However, current LLM-based ABM research faces several key challenges: modeling evolving agent-environment interactions, enabling flexible counterfactual reasoning, and automating simulation workflows for scientific research. In this paper, we propose Eco3S, a socio-economic system simulation framework for economic research and policy analysis that addresses these challenges through three key mechanisms: (1) Co-evolving Environment Design, a bidirectional feedback loop where agents and the environment co-evolve, producing realistic emergent behaviors; (2) Structural Causal Simulation, a structural causal model (SCM)-inspired counterfactual mechanism that allows flexible interventions for diverse causal inference tasks; (3) Simulation-Analysis-Refinement Paradigm, a self-corrective mechanism that iteratively refines experimental designs based on prior simulation results. Experiments on diverse economic scenarios confirm \textit{Eco3S}'s effectiveness in replicating multiple established economic studies (canal decay, origins of governance, and information propagation) and phenomena across domains. Additional results further demonstrate its scalability and generalizability, highlighting the framework's potential for rigorous economic research and policy-making.

</details>


### [243] [Fewer Clarifications, Better Code: Benchmarking Cross-Session Personalized Ambiguity Adaptation in Coding Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26611)
*Zijian Xu,Wenshuo Zhang,Zisen Qin,Rui Sheng,Yushi Sun,Huamin Qu,Chuhan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPA基准，用于研究如何利用同一用户的历史解决会话作为记忆，在新会话中解决重复出现的个性化编码歧义，减少重复澄清需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有消歧方法通常在单个会话中孤立处理每个模糊请求，通过额外澄清来解决，但未探索同一用户的已解决会话历史能否作为记忆在新会话中解决重复出现的个性化歧义。

Method: 将个性化歧义适应形式化为新任务；引入CAPA基准，通过六种机制表征个性化编码歧义，使用三阶段生成流水线将歧义注入清晰可执行任务；提出同用户历史门控的轻量级推断方法。

Result: CAPA包含600个编码会话（60个平衡的用户-歧义单元），其中300个保留用于评估；在12个最新LLM上评估了无历史和有同用户历史条件，分析任务难度、用户身份和基于记忆的历史使用。

Conclusion: CAPA为开发长期编码助手提供了基础，使生成代码更好地与用户意图对齐，同时减少重复澄清。

Abstract: AI-assisted coding increasingly translates informal user intent into executable software, yet coding requests often contain ambiguities that recur in user-specific ways across tasks and sessions. Existing disambiguation methods typically address each ambiguous request in isolation within the current coding session, often through eliciting additional clarification. However, whether resolved session history from the same user can serve as memory for resolving recurring personalized ambiguity in a newly opened session remains underexplored. We formulate personalized ambiguity adaptation as a new task: given a user's previously resolved coding sessions and a new ambiguous request, an assistant should identify the recurring ambiguity pattern, produce the intended executable solution, and minimize clarification. To benchmark this task, we introduce CAPA, which characterizes personalized coding ambiguity through six mechanisms and injects these mechanisms into unambiguous executable tasks using a controlled three-stage generation pipeline. CAPA contains 600 coding sessions across 60 balanced user--ambiguity cells, including 300 held-out evaluation sessions. We evaluate 12 recent LLMs under no-history and same-user-history conditions using executable success, first-turn success, and turns-to-completion. Our analyses examine task difficulty, user identity, and memory-based history use, and we further propose same-user history gating as a lightweight inference-time method. CAPA provides a foundation for developing long-term coding assistants that better align generated code with user intent while reducing repeated clarification.

</details>


### [244] [AlphaSchema: Exploring the Space of Trading Semantics for LLM-Based Alpha Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26642)
*Jingyang Yi,Jian Yang,Yifei Jin,Yuqi Li,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlphaSchema 提出了一种结构化语义空间（Event, Context, Qualities, Direction, Output），将因子探索与实现解耦，通过代理模型和迭代选择机制在语义空间中高效搜索高收益因子。实验表明该方法在股票市场中表现优异，且因子质量对LLM选择具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的因子挖掘系统中，因子构建和搜索决策完全交由LLM自主完成，缺乏显式的探索空间和系统化的搜索机制，导致探索过程隐式且难以控制和优化。

Method: 构建结构化语义空间（Event, Context, Qualities, Direction, Output五元组），将探索与实现分离；LLM将选中的schema计划转化为可执行因子；利用评估奖励学习语义空间上的代理模型，通过全局探索、代理引导利用和局部变异三者平衡的迭代选择机制进行搜索。

Result: 在中国股市实验中，AlphaSchema发现的因子池具有强预测能力和组合表现；语义搜索能遍历多样区域并逐步聚焦高收益区域；不同LLM实现同一schema计划的因子质量相当。

Conclusion: AlphaSchema通过结构化语义空间和代理模型有效实现了因子挖掘的显式、可控探索，且框架对底层LLM选择具有鲁棒性，因子质量主要取决于语义设计而非实现模型。

Abstract: Automated alpha mining has increasingly adopted large language model (LLM) agents for factor generation and iterative discovery. However, existing LLM-based systems often delegate both factor construction and search decisions to the agent itself, without an explicit exploration space or a principled mechanism for navigating that space. As a result, exploration remains largely implicit and difficult to control or optimize systematically. We introduce AlphaSchema, which constructs and explores a structured space of trading semantics for alpha mining. Each point in this space is a schema plan composed of Event, Context, Qualities, Direction, and Output, specifying the semantics of a candidate factor before implementation. AlphaSchema decouples exploration from implementation: an LLM translates selected schema plans into executable factors, while evaluated rewards are accumulated to learn a surrogate model over the semantic space. An iterative selection mechanism uses this model to balance global exploration, surrogate-guided exploitation, and local mutation. Experiments on the Chinese stock market show that AlphaSchema discovers factor pools with strong predictive and portfolio performance. Further analyses show that the semantic search process navigates diverse regions while increasingly allocating evaluations toward high-reward regions, and that implementations of the same schema plans by different LLMs exhibit comparable predictive quality, suggesting that alpha mining quality is largely robust to the choice of LLM within our framework.

</details>


### [245] [Rethinking Self-Evolution: A Constrained Exploration-Exploitation Process for Mitigating Skill Overfitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26643)
*Hongqiang Lin,Chao Liu,Xiaofan Bai,Xuan Jin,Yuhong Li,Nenggan Zheng,Xipeng Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillBoost框架，通过结构化利用、先验引导探索和验证接受三个阶段，在LLM代理的技能自我进化中平衡探索与利用，防止过拟合，实验证明其在23个模型-基准配置上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在真实应用中积累和复用经验存在挑战，数据驱动的技能优化容易过拟合到有限的轨迹数据，过度利用导致过拟合当前批次，无约束探索则导致先前已解决问题退化，需要探索-利用权衡的约束搜索。

Method: 提出SkillBoost三阶段框架：1) 结构化利用——将观察到的失败定位到可编辑的技能组件；2) 先验引导探索——利用LLM中的先验知识生成多样化的修复候选；3) 验证接受——仅在改进性能且不超过回归界限时提交候选。

Result: 在23个模型-基准配置上达到SOTA性能，有效缓解过拟合，优于人工编写和LLM生成的技能；迁移实验表明优化后的技能可被其他代理在类似任务中复用。

Conclusion: SkillBoost通过结构化的探索-利用平衡机制，有效解决了LLM代理技能自我进化中的过拟合问题，提升了技能的性能和可迁移性。

Abstract: Enabling large language model (LLM) agents to accumulate and reuse experience from past interactions remains a central challenge in real-world applications. A promising solution is to treat skills as trainable states and optimize them in the same way as model parameters in neural network training. However, data-driven skill optimization is prone to overfitting to the limited trajectories collected from real environments. Overexploiting these trajectories overfits the current batch, while unconstrained exploration causes regression on previously solved cases. This tension motivates a constrained search view of skill self-evolution, governed by an exploration--exploitation trade-off. We propose SkillBoost, a three-stage framework that mitigates both risks: structured exploitation localizes observed failures to editable skill components, prior-guided exploration draws on prior knowledge in the LLM to generate diverse repair candidates, and verified acceptance commits a candidate only when it improves performance within a regression bound. Experiments across 23 model--benchmark configurations show that SkillBoost achieves state-of-the-art performance while mitigating overfitting, outperforming both human-crafted and LLM-generated skills. Transfer experiments further show that optimized skills can be reused by other agents on similar tasks.

</details>


### [246] [AgenticCANN: Automated Ascend C Operator Generation via Knowledge-Augmented Agentic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26661)
*Junhao Qiu,Zidong Wang,Yansong Sun,Zhitong Ma,Ping Guo,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticCANN，一个面向华为Ascend NPU的知识增强型智能体进化框架，用于自动生成Ascend C算子。该方法通过知识编排生成系统和阶段自适应进化策略，在低语料环境下实现了从57%到86%的可行性提升，并在1B Pangu模型上取得最高6.65倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ascend C算子优化对NPU推理性能至关重要，但需要深厚的硬件专业知识。尽管LLM在自动CUDA内核生成上展现了潜力，但Ascend C完全不同的编程模型带来了尚未被探索的独特挑战，现有方法无法直接迁移。

Method: 1) 知识编排生成系统：提供结构化、多层次的领域知识，解决上游可行性瓶颈；2) 阶段自适应智能体进化策略：动态调整LLM交互模式，在生成和进化阶段平衡高探索性的候选发现与高收敛性的性能调优。

Result: 在华为Ascend 910B上，覆盖5种算子模式的6个算子实验中，元素级和归一化算子可行性达90-100%，融合算子达56%；在1B Pangu模型推理内核上实现最高6.65倍加速；知识注入使元素级算子可行性从57%单调提升至86%。

Conclusion: AgenticCANN成功将LLM应用于Ascend C算子自动生成，验证了知识注入的普适性收益（非算子特定），为低语料NPU环境下的算子自动化提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Ascend C operator optimization is critical for NPU (Neural Processing Unit) inference performance but requires deep hardware expertise.While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in automated CUDA kernel generation, the fundamentally different programming model of Ascend C introduces unique challenges that remain unexplored. In this paper, we propose AgenticCANN, a knowledge-augmented agentic evolution framework specifically tailored for automated Ascend C operator synthesis in low-corpus NPU environments.To overcome the severe platform knowledge deficit on unfamiliar hardware, AgenticCANN incorporates a knowledge-orchestrated generation system that delivers structured, multi-level domain insights across the development lifecycle to resolve the upstream feasibility bottleneck.Building on this foundation, it features a stage-adaptive agentic evolution strategy that dynamically aligns LLM interaction modes with specific generation and evolution phases, balancing high-exploration candidate discovery with high-convergence performance tuning.Extensive experiments on Huawei Ascend 910B across six operators spanning five pattern categories demonstrate that our method achieves 90 to 100 percent feasibility on elementwise and normalization operators, 56% on fusion operators, and up to 6.65$\times$ speedup on 1B Pangu model inference kernels. Further analysis reveals that knowledge injection monotonically improves feasibility from 57% to 86% on elementwise operators, demonstrating its general rather than operator-specific benefit.

</details>


### [247] [UrbanDS: A Graph-Guided LLM Multi-Agent System for Data-Intensive Urban Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26724)
*Zhilun Zhou,Jianghao Yu,Yuming Lin,yongjun yang,Sun Yongquan,Depeng Jin,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UrbanDS，一个图引导的LLM多智能体系统，通过构建统一数据集图组织数据技能和关系，解决大规模异构数据场景下的数据科学任务自动化问题，在数据密集型任务上优于现有方法，并已在武汉东西湖区实际部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在数据科学任务中依赖有限的数据集，难以应对大规模、多源、具有复杂时空语义关系的城市数据场景。

Method: 构建统一数据集图，包含Data Profiling Agent构建数据集技能、Relation Agent识别数据集关系；运行时Planner Agent检索相关数据集并生成执行计划，多个Execution Agent通过共享内存协作执行，Report Agent生成可优化的报告。

Result: 在通用和城市基准测试中，UrbanDS在数据密集型任务上持续优于现有数据科学智能体，并在武汉东西湖区城市运营平台部署验证了实际效果。
